Further investigation with in-depth error analysis exposes a fundamental tension in linguistic-feature-based AI text detection: the features that are most discriminative on in-domain data are also the features most susceptible to domain shift, formatting variation, and text-length effects. We believe that this knowledge helps build AI detectors that are robust across different settings. To support replication and practical use, we release an open-source Python package that returns both predictions and instance-level explanations for individual texts.

</details>


### [37] [UniDial-EvalKit: A Unified Toolkit for Evaluating Multi-Faceted Conversational Abilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23160)
*Qi Jia,Haodong Zhao,Dun Pei,Xiujie Song,Shibo Wang,Zijian Chen,Zicheng Zhang,Xiangyang Zhu,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniDial-EvalKit (UDE) 是一个用于评估交互式AI系统的统一评估工具包，通过标准化数据格式、模块化评估流程和一致的评分接口来解决现有评估协议异构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式AI系统评估协议高度异构，在数据集格式、模型接口和评估流程上存在显著差异，严重阻碍了系统化比较。需要统一的评估框架来促进标准化基准测试。

Method: UDE采用整体统一方法：1) 将异构数据格式标准化为通用模式；2) 通过模块化架构简化复杂评估流程；3) 在一致评分接口下对齐指标计算；4) 支持并行生成和评分的大规模评估；5) 基于检查点的缓存机制消除冗余计算。

Result: 在多样化多轮基准测试中验证，UDE不仅通过标准化工作流程和透明日志保证高可复现性，还显著提高了评估效率和可扩展性。完整工具包和评估脚本已公开可用。

Conclusion: UDE为交互式AI系统提供了一个统一的评估工具包，通过标准化数据格式、模块化架构和高效计算机制，解决了现有评估协议的异构性问题，促进了标准化基准测试生态系统的发展，加速了交互式AI的未来突破。

Abstract: Benchmarking AI systems in multi-turn interactive scenarios is essential for understanding their practical capabilities in real-world applications. However, existing evaluation protocols are highly heterogeneous, differing significantly in dataset formats, model interfaces, and evaluation pipelines, which severely impedes systematic comparison. In this work, we present UniDial-EvalKit (UDE), a unified evaluation toolkit for assessing interactive AI systems. The core contribution of UDE lies in its holistic unification: it standardizes heterogeneous data formats into a universal schema, streamlines complex evaluation pipelines through a modular architecture, and aligns metric calculations under a consistent scoring interface. It also supports efficient large-scale evaluation through parallel generation and scoring, as well as checkpoint-based caching to eliminate redundant computation. Validated across diverse multi-turn benchmarks, UDE not only guarantees high reproducibility through standardized workflows and transparent logging, but also significantly improves evaluation efficiency and extensibility. We make the complete toolkit and evaluation scripts publicly available to foster a standardized benchmarking ecosystem and accelerate future breakthroughs in interactive AI.

</details>


### [38] [ImplicitRM: Unbiased Reward Modeling from Implicit Preference Data for LLM alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23184)
*Hao Wang,Haocheng Yang,Licheng Pan,Lei Shen,Xiaoxi Li,Yinuo Wang,Zhichao Chen,Yuan Lu,Haoxuan Li,Zhouchen Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ImplicitRM方法，从隐式人类反馈（如点击、复制）中学习无偏奖励模型，解决了隐式偏好数据缺乏明确负样本和用户偏好偏差的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）中，奖励建模严重依赖实验反馈数据，收集成本高昂。隐式人类反馈（如点击、复制）作为成本效益更高的替代方案，但面临两个基本挑战：缺乏明确负样本和用户偏好偏差。

Method: 提出ImplicitRM方法：1）通过分层模型将训练样本分为四个潜在组；2）基于似然最大化推导学习目标，理论证明该方法是无偏的，有效解决了隐式偏好数据的两个挑战。

Result: 实验表明，ImplicitRM能够在多个隐式偏好数据集上学习到准确的奖励模型。

Conclusion: ImplicitRM为从隐式人类反馈中学习无偏奖励模型提供了一种有效的解决方案，降低了RLHF中奖励建模的数据收集成本。

Abstract: Reward modeling represents a long-standing challenge in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for aligning language models. Current reward modeling is heavily contingent upon experimental feedback data with high collection costs. In this work, we study \textit{implicit reward modeling} -- learning reward models from implicit human feedback (e.g., clicks and copies) -- as a cost-effective alternative. We identify two fundamental challenges in implicit reward modeling: (1) Implicit preference data lacks definitive negative samples, which makes standard positive-negative classification methods inapplicable; (2) Implicit preference data suffers from user preference bias, where different responses have different propensities to elicit user feedback actions, which exacerbates the difficulty of distinguishing definitive negative samples. To address these challenges, we propose ImplicitRM, which aims to learn unbiased reward models from implicit preference data. ImplicitRM stratifies training samples into four latent groups via a stratification model. Building on this, it derives a learning objective through likelihood maximization, which we prove is theoretically unbiased, effectively resolving both challenges. Experiments demonstrate that ImplicitRM learns accurate reward models across implicit preference datasets. Code is available on our project website.

</details>


### [39] [Is AI Catching Up to Human Expression? Exploring Emotion, Personality, Authorship, and Linguistic Style in English and Arabic with Six Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23251)
*Nasser A Alsadhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在模拟人类情感和人格特质方面存在显著差异，AI生成文本与人类文本在情感表达、人格标记和语言特征上可区分，尤其在阿拉伯语等资源匮乏语言中表现明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs流畅度的提升，需要研究它们在不同语言和文化背景下模拟复杂人类特质（如情感表达和人格）的能力，特别是在阿拉伯语等资源匮乏语言中，这些语言具有独特的语言和文化特征。

Method: 研究使用六个模型（Jais、Mistral、LLaMA、GPT-4o、Gemini、DeepSeek）进行两项任务：1）评估机器学习分类器能否可靠区分人类撰写和AI生成文本；2）评估LLM生成文本在情感或人格特质方面与人类文本的相似程度。

Result: AI生成文本与人类文本可区分（F1>0.95），但分类性能在改写样本上下降，表明依赖表面风格线索。情感和人格分类实验显示显著泛化差距：在人类数据上训练的分类器在AI生成文本上表现差，反之亦然，表明LLMs以不同于人类的方式编码情感信号。增强AI生成数据训练可提升阿拉伯语人格分类性能。GPT-4o和Gemini表现出优越的情感连贯性。语言和心理语言分析显示人类与AI文本在语调、真实性和文本复杂性上存在可测量差异。

Conclusion: LLMs在模拟人类情感和人格特质方面与人类存在系统性差异，这些发现对情感计算、作者归属和负责任AI部署具有重要影响，特别是在资源匮乏语言环境中，生成式AI检测和对齐面临独特挑战。合成数据在解决资源匮乏语言挑战方面具有潜力。

Abstract: The advancing fluency of LLMs raises important questions about their ability to emulate complex human traits, including emotional expression and personality, across diverse linguistic and cultural contexts. This study investigates whether LLMs can convincingly mimic emotional nuance in English and personality markers in Arabic, a critical under-resourced language with unique linguistic and cultural characteristics. We conduct two tasks across six models:Jais, Mistral, LLaMA, GPT-4o, Gemini, and DeepSeek. First, we evaluate whether machine classifiers can reliably distinguish between human-authored and AI-generated texts. Second, we assess the extent to which LLM-generated texts exhibit emotional or personality traits comparable to those of humans. Our results demonstrate that AI-generated texts are distinguishable from human-authored ones (F1>0.95), though classification performance deteriorates on paraphrased samples, indicating a reliance on superficial stylistic cues. Emotion and personality classification experiments reveal significant generalization gaps: classifiers trained on human data perform poorly on AI-generated texts and vice versa, suggesting LLMs encode affective signals differently from humans. Importantly, augmenting training with AI-generated data enhances performance in the Arabic personality classification task, highlighting the potential of synthetic data to address challenges in under-resourced languages. Model-specific analyses show that GPT-4o and Gemini exhibit superior affective coherence. Linguistic and psycholinguistic analyses reveal measurable divergences in tone, authenticity, and textual complexity between human and AI texts. These findings have implications for affective computing, authorship attribution, and responsible AI deployment, particularly within underresourced language contexts where generative AI detection and alignment pose unique challenges.

</details>


### [40] [Steering LLMs for Culturally Localized Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23301)
*Simran Khanuja,Hongbin Liu,Shujian Zhang,John Lambert,Mingqing Chen,Rajiv Mathews,Lun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用稀疏自编码器识别LLM中的文化特征，构建文化嵌入(CuE)来分析和操纵文化偏见，提供可解释的文化表征诊断和白盒干预方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM存在文化偏见，偏向训练数据丰富的文化；现有文化本地化方法（如提示或后训练对齐）是黑盒、难以控制，且无法区分失败是由于知识缺失还是激发策略不当

Method: 使用机制可解释性方法，通过稀疏自编码器识别编码文化显著信息的可解释特征，将其聚合成文化嵌入(CuE)，用于分析隐含文化偏见和构建白盒干预

Result: CuE干预提高了文化忠实度，比单纯提示更能激发罕见、长尾文化概念；CuE与黑盒本地化方法互补，在提示增强输入基础上仍有增益；模型确实受益于更好的激发策略，不一定缺乏长尾知识表征

Conclusion: 研究为LLM中的文化表征提供了诊断洞察，并提供了可控的方法来引导模型朝向目标文化，揭示了模型可能具备但未被充分激发的文化知识

Abstract: LLMs are deployed globally, yet produce responses biased towards cultures with abundant training data. Existing cultural localization approaches such as prompting or post-training alignment are black-box, hard to control, and do not reveal whether failures reflect missing knowledge or poor elicitation. In this paper, we address these gaps using mechanistic interpretability to uncover and manipulate cultural representations in LLMs. Leveraging sparse autoencoders, we identify interpretable features that encode culturally salient information and aggregate them into Cultural Embeddings (CuE). We use CuE both to analyze implicit cultural biases under underspecified prompts and to construct white-box steering interventions. Across multiple models, we show that CuE-based steering increases cultural faithfulness and elicits significantly rarer, long-tail cultural concepts than prompting alone. Notably, CuE-based steering is complementary to black-box localization methods, offering gains when applied on top of prompt-augmented inputs. This also suggests that models do benefit from better elicitation strategies, and don't necessarily lack long-tail knowledge representation, though this varies across cultures. Our results provide both diagnostic insight into cultural representations in LLMs and a controllable method to steer towards desired cultures.

</details>


### [41] [WISTERIA: Weak Implicit Signal-based Temporal Relation Extraction with Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23319)
*Duy Dao Do,Anaïs Halftermeyer,Thi-Bich-Hanh Dao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WISTERIA框架通过分析事件对特定注意力机制，从隐式信号中提取时间关系，提供可解释的局部推理视图


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力的模型通常关注全局显著标记，但忽略了决定时间关系的特定事件对线索。需要一种能够捕捉隐式时间顺序信号并提供可解释证据的方法。

Method: 提出WISTERIA框架，结合多头注意力与事件对条件化的top-K池化，为每个事件对隔离最具信息量的上下文标记，考虑词汇、句法或形态学上的隐式时间顺序表达。

Result: 在TimeBank-Dense、MATRES、TDDMan和TDDAuto数据集上实验表明，WISTERIA达到竞争性准确率，并能揭示与时间语言学线索对齐的事件对层面理据。

Conclusion: WISTERIA通过分析事件对特定注意力组件，提供了一种局部化和可解释的时间推理方法，能够有效捕捉隐式时间信号并提高模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Temporal Relation Extraction (TRE) requires identifying how two events or temporal expressions are related in time. Existing attention-based models often highlight globally salient tokens but overlook the pair-specific cues that actually determine the temporal relation. We propose WISTERIA (Weak Implicit Signal-based Temporal Relation Extraction with Attention), a framework that examines whether the top-K attention components conditioned on each event pair truly encode interpretable evidence for temporal classification. Unlike prior works assuming explicit markers such as before, after, or when, WISTERIA considers signals as any lexical, syntactic, or morphological element implicitly expressing temporal order. By combining multi-head attention with pair-conditioned top-K pooling, the model isolates the most informative contextual tokens for each pair. We conduct extensive experiments on TimeBank-Dense, MATRES, TDDMan, and TDDAuto, including linguistic analyses of top-K tokens. Results show that WISTERIA achieves competitive accuracy and reveals pair-level rationales aligned with temporal linguistic cues, offering a localized and interpretable view of temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [42] [Failure of contextual invariance in gender inference with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23485)
*Sagar Kumar,Ariel Flint,Luca Maria Aiello,Andrea Baronchelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在性别推断任务中的输出对微小的、理论上无信息的语境变化敏感，违反语境不变性假设，影响偏见基准测试和高风险部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准评估实践中关于LLM输出在语境等价的任务表述下保持稳定的假设，特别是在性别推断任务中，探究微小语境变化如何影响模型行为。

Method: 使用受控的代词选择任务，引入最小化、理论上无信息的语篇语境，分析语境变化对模型输出的影响。采用Contextuality-by-Default分析来量化语境依赖性。

Result: 1. 微小语境变化导致模型输出出现大规模系统性偏移；2. 去语境化设置中存在的文化性别刻板印象相关性在引入语境后减弱或消失；3. 理论上无关的特征（如无关指代词的性别）成为模型行为的最具预测性因素；4. 19-52%的案例中，语境依赖性在考虑所有边际效应后仍然存在，不能归因于简单的代词重复。

Conclusion: LLM输出即使在近乎相同的句法表述下也违反语境不变性，这对偏见基准测试和高风险部署具有重要影响，表明当前评估实践需要重新考虑语境敏感性。

Abstract: Standard evaluation practices assume that large language model (LLM) outputs are stable under contextually equivalent formulations of a task. Here, we test this assumption in the setting of gender inference. Using a controlled pronoun selection task, we introduce minimal, theoretically uninformative discourse context and find that this induces large, systematic shifts in model outputs. Correlations with cultural gender stereotypes, present in decontextualized settings, weaken or disappear once context is introduced, while theoretically irrelevant features, such as the gender of a pronoun for an unrelated referent, become the most informative predictors of model behaviour. A Contextuality-by-Default analysis reveals that, in 19--52\% of cases across models, this dependence persists after accounting for all marginal effects of context on individual outputs and cannot be attributed to simple pronoun repetition. These findings show that LLM outputs violate contextual invariance even under near-identical syntactic formulations, with implications for bias benchmarking and deployment in high-stakes settings.

</details>

### [43] [Cognitive Training for Language Models: Towards General Capabilities via Cross-Entropy Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22479)
*Clément Hongler,Franck Gabriel,Valentin Hartmann,Arthur Renard,Andrew Emil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出认知训练框架，通过交叉熵游戏构建任务课程，实现语言模型能力的自动增长和技能发现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语言模型通用能力自动构建的开放问题，通过相关技能发现的课程学习实现模型能力的逐步增长

Method: 提出认知训练框架，使用交叉熵游戏作为任务族，通过贪婪优化算法迭代构建课程，实现相关技能发现

Result: 证明在自然假设下，通过贪婪优化构建课程的方法本质上只有一种可能的元目标（除少数超参数外）

Conclusion: 认知训练为相关技能发现提供了原则性方法，如果通用能力可通过贪婪课程学习实现，认知训练将是解决方案

Abstract: Defining a constructive process to build general capabilities for language models in an automatic manner is considered an open problem in artificial intelligence. Towards this, we consider the problem of building a curriculum of tasks that grows a model via relevant skill discovery. We provide a concrete framework for this task, using a family of tasks called cross-entropy games, which we postulate is universal in a suitable sense. We show that if it is possible to grow the curriculum for relevant skill discovery by iterating a greedy optimization algorithm, then, under natural assumptions, there is essentially only one meta-objective possible (up to a few hyperparameters). We call the resulting process cognitive training. We postulate that, given sufficiently capable language models as players and meta-samplers and sufficient training time, cognitive training provides a principled way to relevant skill discovery; and hence to the extent general capabilities are achievable via greedy curriculum learning, cognitive training would be a solution.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多目标拓扑优化通常基于离散的帕累托前沿近似，缺乏对问题几何结构的深入理解。本文旨在建立变分框架，从几何角度重新解释多目标优化问题，揭示帕累托前沿的内在结构，并提供更稳定、均匀的近似方法。

Method: 1. 将水平集函数视为虚构材料的广义坐标；2. 通过哈密顿原理推导运动方程，得到阻尼波方程控制优化过程；3. 使用加权和方法组合目标函数；4. 分析系统结构，揭示问题的几何解释；5. 引入权重参数的动态演化，形成水平集函数与权重的耦合动力系统。

Result: 1. 在适当正则性假设下，平稳解集在目标空间中形成结构化子集，帕累托前沿局部嵌入其中，权重因子作为内在坐标；2. 数值结果表明，该框架能提供稳定且均匀的帕累托前沿近似；3. 方法可扩展到高维目标空间。

Conclusion: 提出的变分框架为多目标水平集拓扑优化提供了新的几何视角，通过耦合动力系统实现目标空间的自适应探索，相比传统离散近似方法，能获得更稳定、均匀的帕累托前沿近似，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a variational framework for multi-objective level set topology optimization. The approach interprets the level set function as a generalized coordinate of a fictitious material and derives its equation of motion from Hamilton's principle, resulting in a damped wave equation governing the optimization process. The objective functionals are combined using a weighted sum formulation. An analysis of the underlying system structure reveals a geometric interpretation of the problem, shifting the perspective beyond conventional approaches based on purely discrete approximations of the Pareto frontier. Under suitable regularity assumptions, the set of stationary solutions forms a structured subset in objective space, in which the Pareto frontier is locally embedded and the weighting factors act as intrinsic coordinates. This perspective motivates the introduction of a dynamic evolution of the weights, leading to a coupled dynamical system for the level set function and the weighting parameters that enables adaptive exploration of the objective landscape. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a stable and uniform approximation of the Pareto frontier and scales to higher-dimensional objective spaces.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是扩展对有限时间和固定时间收敛的理解，特别是放宽传统Lyapunov方法中对可微、正定Lyapunov函数的要求，探索不同收敛速率之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用LaSalle类不变性原理，提出了一阶和二阶非光滑Lyapunov类结果，通过系统缩放技术将渐近稳定系统转化为固定时间稳定系统。

Result: 建立了有限时间和固定时间收敛的新理论框架，证明了全局渐近稳定的平衡点可以通过适当的缩放变换成为固定时间稳定的平衡点，揭示了不同收敛速率之间的转换关系。

Conclusion: 论文扩展了对各种收敛速率的理解，强化了"所有收敛速率通过适当变换相互关联"的假设，为非光滑系统的有限时间和固定时间稳定性分析提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we present new results on finite- and fixed-time convergence for dynamical systems using LaSalle-like invariance principles. In particular, we provide first and second-order non-smooth Lyapunov-like results for finite- and fixed-time convergence, thereby relaxing the requirement of existence a differentiable, positive definite Lyapunov function. Based on these findings, we show that a dynamical system whose equilibrium point is globally asymptotically stable can be modified through scaling so that the resulting dynamical system has a fixed-time stable equilibrium point. The results in this paper expand our understanding of various convergence rates and strengthen the hypothesis that all the convergence rates are interconnected through a suitable transformation.

</details>


### [46] [Quantifying Non-linearity in Topology Optimization with similarity based Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22889)
*Ziliang Wang,Jiahua Wu,Jun Yang,Shintaro Yamasaki*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出可视化框架和定量非线性指数，用于分析拓扑优化问题的非线性复杂性，指导参数选择和稳定策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑优化中，弱非线性问题（如柔度最小化）的基于灵敏度方法通常收敛良好，而强非线性问题（如最大应力最小化）需要稳定策略来增强收敛。在大量设计变量场景下，难以直观展示不同拓扑优化问题的非线性复杂性，也难以阐明稳定策略如何影响收敛机制。

Method: 提出可视化框架和定量非线性指数：1) 采用多起点固定梯度采样，针对基于相似性的降维进行定制，控制计算成本；2) 通过余弦相似度对样本进行参数化，获得目标函数的低维可视化曲面；3) 基于可视化构建无量纲复杂度指数，通过测量可视化曲面与其凸包离散近似之间的间隙来获得清晰的几何解释。

Result: 广泛的比较实验表明，所提出的方法在各种代表性拓扑优化问题上既具有适应性又具有区分性，为参数选择提供了直观且可测量的指导。

Conclusion: 该框架能够定量比较不同拓扑优化任务、参数选择和稳定策略之间的非线性，为解决拓扑优化中非线性复杂性分析提供了有效的可视化工具和量化指标。

Abstract: Topology optimization (TO) can be viewed as seeking an optimal solution in the design space of a given TO problem. For weakly non-linear TO problems, e.g., compliance minimization, sensitivity-based methods typically converge well, whereas for strongly non-linear problems, e.g., maximum stress minimization, stabilization strategies such as stabilization terms and projection functions are often required to enhance convergence. Especially in scenarios with massive design variables, it is difficult to intuitively demonstrate the non-linear complexity of different TO problems and to elucidate the mechanisms by which stabilization strategies affect convergence. To address this challenge, we propose a visualization framework and a quantitative non-linearity index for objectives with varying complexity. We employ a multi-start fixed-gradient sampling tailored to similarity-based dimensionality reduction while keeping the computational cost under control. The samples are then parameterized via cosine similarity to obtain a low-dimensional visualization surface of the objective function. Based on this visualization, we construct a dimensionless complexity index with a clear geometric interpretation by measuring the gap between the visualization surface and the discrete approximation of its convex envelope, which enables quantitative comparisons of non-linearity across TO tasks, parameter choices, and stabilization strategies. Extensive comparative experiments show that the proposed approach is both adaptable and discriminative on a variety of representative TO problems, and it provides intuitive and measurable guidance for parameter selection.

</details>

*David Ohlin,Anders Rantzer,Emma Tegling*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究认为正线性系统无法使用正Luenberger型观测器稳定，但本文旨在证明这种稳定是可能的，并扩展观测器设计条件，同时将结果应用于非正系统。

Method: 将观测器构造为状态的上界和下界单调收敛，分析线性观测器反馈下的闭环特性，推导出比先前设计更广泛的条件。对非正系统通过上界观测器反馈强制动态正性，扩展到随机噪声情况。

Result: 成功证明正线性系统可以使用正Luenberger型观测器稳定，给出了比先前设计更广泛的稳定条件，能够处理非正系统和随机噪声情况，在期望意义下保证收敛。

Conclusion: 正线性系统确实可以通过正Luenberger型观测器稳定，通过构造单调收敛的上下界观测器实现。该方法扩展了观测器设计条件，可应用于非正系统和随机环境，具有理论和实际意义。

Abstract: The paper shows that positive linear systems can be stabilized using positive Luenberger-type observers, contradicting previous conclusions. This is achieved by structuring the observer as monotonically converging upper and lower bounds on the state. Analysis of the closed-loop properties under linear observer feedback gives conditions that cover a larger class than previous observer designs. The results are applied to nonpositive systems by enforcing positivity of the dynamics using feedback from the upper bound observer. The setting is expanded to include stochastic noise, giving conditions for convergence in expectation using feedback from positive observers.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a structure-aware optimization problem for decision diagrams used for health guidance. In particular, we focus on decision diagrams that decide to whom public sectors suggest consulting a medical worker. Furthermore, these diagrams decide which notification method should be used for each target person. In this paper, we formulate this problem as an integer program. Then we evaluate its practical usefulness through numerical examples.

### [49] [Minimizing Material Waste in Additive Manufacturing through Online Reel Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23042)
*Ilayda Celenk,Willem van Jaarsveld,Ivo J. B. F. Adan,Alp Akcay*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 在线装箱问题变体在3D打印中的应用：有限容量线轴分配问题，通过马尔可夫决策过程建模，结合理论索引策略与深度强化学习减少材料浪费


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基于线轴的3D打印系统中的在线装箱问题，系统在按订单生产环境中只能同时处理有限数量的有限容量线轴。组件到达时分配给线轴，容量不足的线轴被丢弃替换为新线轴，导致材料浪费。需要最小化长期平均丢弃的线材

Method: 将问题建模为无限时域平均成本马尔可夫决策过程。首先分析随机分配策略下系统分解为相同单线轴过程，得到平均浪费的闭式表达式。基于此分解构建理论基础的索引策略，为每个线轴分配反映分配边际成本的分数，证明这是对随机分配的一步策略改进。将索引结构嵌入深度强化学习框架，使用近似策略迭代方法

Result: 随机分配策略提供了可处理的基线分析。索引策略在理论上优于随机分配。结合深度强化学习的方法在模拟和实际场景中实现了接近最优的性能，显著减少材料浪费，同时保持实时可行性和可解释性

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种结合理论索引策略与深度强化学习的混合方法，有效解决了3D打印系统中的在线线轴分配问题。该方法在减少材料浪费方面表现出色，同时保持了实时计算可行性和策略的可解释性，为实际工业应用提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: We study a variant of the online bin packing problem that arises in filament-based 3D printing systems operating in make-to-order settings, where only a limited number of filament reels of finite capacity can be handled at once. Components are assigned to reels upon arrival and insufficient reels are discarded to be replaced with new ones, resulting in material waste. To minimize the long-run average discarded filament through an online assignment policy, we formulate this problem as an infinite-horizon average-cost Markov Decision Process and analyze the structure of policies under stochastic, sequential demand. We first show that under a random allocation policy, the system decomposes into a collection of identical single-reel processes, allowing us to derive a closed-form expression for the average waste and enabling a tractable baseline analysis. Building on this decomposition, we construct a theoretically grounded index policy that assigns each reel a score reflecting the marginal cost of assignment and prove that it constitutes a one-step policy improvement over random allocation. We embed the index-based structure within a Deep Reinforcement Learning framework using approximate policy iteration. The resulting method achieves near-optimal performance across a range of simulated and real-world scenarios. Our results demonstrate that Reinforcement Learning policy significantly reduces material waste while maintaining real-time feasibility and interpretability.

</details>

*Geng-Hua Li,Hai-Yi Zhao,Xiangkai Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对具有"非光滑+非光滑"复合结构的线性等式约束凸优化问题，研究基于混合阶动力系统的时间尺度与Tikhonov正则化算法，提出两种乘子类型对应的新算法并分析其收敛性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有"非光滑+非光滑"复合结构的线性等式约束凸优化问题，传统方法可能效率不高或收敛性不足，需要开发新的高效算法来处理这类非可分离问题

Method: 基于混合阶动力系统，结合时间尺度和Tikhonov正则化项，针对隐式乘子和半隐式乘子分别开发了新的联合原始-对偶算法和分裂算法

Result: 建立了所有提出算法的非遍历收敛性质，证明算法生成的序列强收敛到最小范数解，数值实验验证了算法的实际性能

Conclusion: 提出的算法能有效处理"非光滑+非光滑"复合结构的线性约束凸优化问题，具有强收敛性和实际应用价值，其中联合算法还可推广到相应的非可分离问题

Abstract: For a linear equality constrained convex optimization problem involving two objective functions with a ``nonsmooth" + ``nonsmooth" composite structure, we study two algorithms derived from a mixed-order dynamical system which incorporates time scales and a Tikhonov regularization term. We observe that different types of multipliers lead to distinct algorithms. For the implicit multiplier and semi-implicit multiplier, we develop a new primal-dual joint algorithm and a new splitting algorithm, respectively. Our proposed joint algorithm can reduce to an algorithm for solving the corresponding non-separable linearly constrained convex optimization problem. Then, we establish the nonergodic convergence properties of all our proposed algorithms. Moreover, we derive that the sequences generated by these algorithms strongly converge to the minimal norm solution. Finally, numerical experiments are conducted to validate the practical performance of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [51] [The relief distribution problem with trucks and drones under incomplete demand information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23135)
*Aaron Neugebauer,Alena Otto,Marie Schmidt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究灾害救援中无人机辅助的应急物资配送问题，通过理论分析和计算实验评估两种策略的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害救援行动通常在受损程度不确定的情况下进行，需要高效分配救援物资。无人机可以侦察需求信息，但如何有效部署无人机以最小化救援完成时间是一个重要问题

Method: 引入救援配送问题(RDP)的形式化模型，研究两种自然策略：竞争比分析（与完全信息最优解比较）和无人机影响分析（与无无人机情况比较）。通过理论分析和计算实验评估策略性能

Result: 通过理论分析和计算实验，量化了两种策略在竞争比和无人机影响方面的性能，揭示了无人机部署的操作权衡

Conclusion: 研究为灾害响应中无人机的有效部署提供了理论依据和操作见解，有助于优化救援物资配送策略

Abstract: Disaster relief operations often take place under uncertainty regarding the extent of damage across locations. In this paper, we study the delivery of relief aid in the aftermath of disasters when delivery vehicles are assisted by surveillance drones and the demand for relief supplies is initially unknown. We introduce a stylized problem that arises in many emergency supply delivery settings -- the relief distribution problem (RDP). In RDP, emergency vehicles, referred to as trucks, must distribute relief supplies on a network, starting from the depot to potential delivery locations, whose demand is initially unknown. The trucks are assisted by surveillance drones, which cannot deliver relief supplies, but scout delivery locations to see whether relief supplies are needed or not. The objective is to visit all location by any vehicle, deliver supplies to all damaged ones, and minimizing the completion time of the relief operation. We study two natural policies for the online problem RDP which we evaluate in two ways: the competitive ratio quantifies the performance in comparison to an optimal solution obtained under full information on damages, the drone-impact is the ratio of the algorithm's performance to the best outcome achievable without drones. Through theoretical analysis and computational experiments, we characterize the operational trade-offs between these policies and derive insights for the effective deployment of drones in disaster response.

</details>


### [52] [Mean Field Games for Renewable Energy Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23156)
*Luciano Campi,Zhuoshu Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一个平均场博弈框架来建模竞争性电力市场中可再生能源生产的演化，分析生产者互动、最优控制、均衡价格，并扩展到包含社会规划者的Stackelberg博弈，通过深度学习数值方法研究政策干预对产能动态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究竞争性电力市场中可再生能源生产的演化动态，需要建模生产者在现货价格互动下的利润优化问题，考虑生产、安装、产能调整成本以及发电不确定性，分析市场均衡和政策干预效果。

Method: 首先将市场建模为N玩家随机微分博弈，分析其平均场博弈极限；通过随机最大值原理推导前向-后向随机微分方程表征最优控制；扩展到Stackelberg平均场博弈包含社会规划者；使用深度学习数值方案近似均衡。

Result: 建立了FBSDE解的存在唯一性，证明了平均场博弈存在唯一均衡；证明了扩展HJB系统粘性解的存在唯一性；数值分析显示最优补贴设计依赖于市场条件，能缓解产能短缺和过剩问题。

Conclusion: 平均场博弈框架能有效建模可再生能源市场的演化动态，Stackelberg扩展能分析政策干预效果，最优补贴设计需考虑市场条件，深度学习数值方法为复杂均衡分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We propose a mean field game (MFG) framework to model the evolution of renewable energy production in competitive electricity markets. Producers interact through the spot price while optimising their profits under production, installation, and capacity adjustment costs, as well as the generation uncertainty. We first formulate the market as an $N$-player stochastic differential game and analyse its mean field game limit as $N\to\infty$. We characterise the representative producer's optimal control via forward-backward stochastic differential equations (FBSDEs) derived from the stochastic maximum principle and determine the corresponding equilibrium spot price. We establish existence and uniqueness of solutions to the FBSDEs and prove that the MFG admits a unique equilibrium. We then extend the model to a Stackelberg mean field game to incorporate the role of a social planner. The planner's optimisation problem leads to an extended Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB) system, for which we prove existence and uniqueness of viscosity solutions. Finally, we implement a deep learning-based numerical scheme to approximate the equilibrium and investigate the impact of policy interventions on capacity dynamics. Our results highlight how optimal subsidy design depends on prevailing market conditions and can mitigate both capacity shortages and overproduction.

</details>


### [53] [Superlinear convergence in nonsmooth optimization via higher-order cutting-plane models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23236)
*Bennet Gebken,Michael Ulbrich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种用于非光滑优化的高阶割平面模型，并基于此构建了信赖域束方法，实现了局部超线性收敛


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在光滑优化中，使用高阶模型可以实现超线性收敛。本文旨在证明在非光滑优化中，通过使用高阶割平面模型同样可以实现超线性收敛

Method: 首先为lower-C^2函数形式化定义高阶割平面模型并推导误差估计，然后构建基于这些模型的信赖域束方法

Result: 证明了该方法能够实现严重步的局部超线性收敛，对于某些有限max型函数能够实现整体超线性收敛，并通过数值实验验证了超线性收敛性

Conclusion: 高阶割平面模型可以成功应用于非光滑优化，实现超线性收敛，扩展了光滑优化中的高阶收敛理论到非光滑领域

Abstract: A cutting-plane model for a nonsmooth function is the maximum of several first-order expansions centered at different points. Using such a model in a bundle method leads to linear convergence (of serious steps) to a minimum. In smooth optimization, superlinear convergence can be achieved by using higher-order models. We show that the same is true for the nonsmooth case, i.e., we show that cutting-plane models involving higher-order expansions can be used to achieve superlinear convergence in nonsmooth optimization. We first formally define higher-order cutting-plane models for lower-$C^2$ functions and derive an error estimate. Afterwards, we construct a trust-region bundle method based on these models that achieves local superlinear convergence of serious steps, and overall superlinear convergence for certain finite max-type functions. Finally, we verify the superlinear convergence in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [54] [Enclosing minima in nonsmooth optimization via trust regions of higher-order cutting-plane models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23261)
*Bennet Gebken,Michael Ulbrich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种全局收敛的信任域束方法，用于最小化低阶C^2函数，使用高阶割平面模型，能够在目标函数满足特定增长假设时计算包含最小值的递减信任域


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要开发能够处理低阶C^2函数（如有限最大值型函数）的全局收敛优化方法，这些函数在机器学习、工程优化等领域常见但传统方法难以有效处理

Method: 基于高阶割平面模型的信任域束方法，通过构建高阶近似模型，在满足特定增长条件时计算包含最小值的递减信任域序列

Result: 方法在目标函数满足尖锐或二次增长条件时全局收敛，能够计算包含最小值的递减信任域，为局部超线性收敛方法提供良好初始化

Conclusion: 该方法为低阶C^2函数优化提供了有效的全局收敛框架，特别适用于有限最大值型函数，通过信任域机制为局部快速收敛方法提供可靠初始化

Abstract: We propose a globally convergent trust-region bundle method for minimizing lower-$C^2$ functions using higher-order cutting-plane models. Under certain growth assumptions on the objective around its minimum, the method is able to compute infinitely many trust regions of decreasing size that contain the minimum. We show that these growth assumptions are satisfied for certain finite max-type functions with sharp or quadratic growth. Enclosing the minimum in this way can be used to initialize local superlinearly convergent methods, which we demonstrate in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [55] [Exponential Turnpike Theorems for Nonlinear Deterministic Meanfield Optimal Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23303)
*Benoît Bonnet-Weill,Giovanni Colombo,Denis Shishmintsev,Emmanuel Trélat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文针对非线性确定性平均场最优控制问题，在拉格朗日和欧拉两种框架下建立了指数转向定理，通过二阶哈密顿展开和算子Riccati对角化等方法证明了最优轨线在长时间控制问题中指数收敛于稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非线性确定性平均场最优控制问题的长时间行为，特别是在拉格朗日和欧拉两种数学框架下，建立指数转向定理以描述最优控制轨线在长时间尺度下收敛于稳态的性质。

Method: 1. 在拉格朗日框架下：将问题提升到随机变量的希尔伯特空间，结合一阶最优性条件、提升哈密顿量的二阶展开和算子Riccati对角化论证；2. 在欧拉框架下：推导静态约束问题的内在KKT条件，分析欧拉二阶假设如何分解为水平部分（通过酉共轭转移到提升空间）和垂直部分（简化为乘法算子上的一致点镇定性与可检测性条件）。

Result: 1. 在拉格朗日框架下证明了指数转向定理；2. 在欧拉框架下证明了Wasserstein空间中Pontryagin三元组的指数转向定理；3. 阐明了Wasserstein Hessian与其拉格朗日提升之间的联系，并澄清了占据测度、局部欧拉极小化子和控制约束在结果中的作用。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了非线性确定性平均场最优控制问题的指数转向定理，统一了拉格朗日和欧拉两种框架的分析方法，为长时间最优控制问题的稳态行为提供了严格的理论基础，并揭示了两种数学表述之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: In this article, we establish exponential turnpike theorems for a class of nonlinear deterministic meanfield optimal control problems. We carry out our analysis simultaneously in the so-called Lagrangian and Eulerian frameworks. In the Lagrangian setting, the problem is lifted to a Hilbert space of random variables, and we prove an exponential turnpike theorem by combining first-order optimality conditions, a second-order expansion of the lifted Hamiltonian, and an operator Riccati diagonalization argument. In the Eulerian setting, we derive intrinsic KKT conditions for the static constrained problem, and show how the Eulerian second-order hypotheses split into a horizontal part, transferred by unitary conjugation to the lifted space, and a vertical part which reduces to uniform pointwise stabilizability and detectability conditions on multiplication operators. This yields an exponential turnpike theorem in the Wasserstein space for optimal Pontryagin triples. Along the way, we %provide explicit the link between Wasserstein Hessians and their Lagrangian lifts, and provide several remarks clarifying the role of occupation measures, local Eulerian minimizers, and control constraints in our results.

</details>


### [56] [Piecewise M-Stationarity and Related Algorithms for Mathematical Programs with Complementarity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23389)
*Kexin Wang,Lorenz T. Biegler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了互补约束数学规划(MPCC)的B-平稳性及算法收敛行为，特别关注双活跃互补约束情况，提出了分段M-平稳性概念并证明其在MPCC-ACQ下等价于B-平稳性，分析了NCP基边界方法的收敛特性，降低了B-平稳性验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究MPCC中B-平稳性的验证问题，特别是双活跃互补约束情况下的复杂性，旨在降低B-平稳性验证的计算成本，并分析算法收敛到非强平稳解时的实际问题。

Method: 提出分段M-平稳性概念并证明其在MPCC-ACQ下与B-平稳性等价；分析NCP基边界方法的收敛特性，无需MPCC-LICQ条件；结合分段M-平稳性概念解释算法行为以降低验证成本；讨论收敛到非强平稳解的实际问题。

Result: 证明了分段M-平稳性与B-平稳性在MPCC-ACQ下的等价性；NCP基边界方法在无需MPCC-LICQ条件下具有良好的收敛特性；NCP基互补重构在避免非强平稳解附近乘子无界方面具有优势；显著降低了B-平稳性验证的计算成本。

Conclusion: 分段M-平稳性为MPCC的B-平稳性验证提供了有效工具，NCP基方法在收敛性和避免乘子无界方面具有优势，为MPCC算法的实际应用提供了理论支持和改进方向。

Abstract: This study explores B-stationarity of mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) and convergence behavior of MPCC algorithms. Special attention is given to the cases with biactive complementarity constraints. First, we propose the concept of piecewise M-stationarity and prove its equivalence to B-stationarity under MPCC-ACQ. Then, we investigate convergence properties of the NCP-based bounding methods we proposed in [31], without requiring MPCC-LICQ; an interpretation of the algorithm's behavior together with the concept of piecewise M-stationarity leads to a cost reduction in B-stationarity verification. In addition, practical issues related to convergence to non-strongly stationary solutions are discussed, which shows that the NCP-based complementarity reformulations have an advantage in avoiding unbounded multipliers near these solutions.

</details>


### [57] [Universal and Parameter-free Gradient Sliding for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23492)
*Yan Wu,Yuyuan Ouyang,Zhe Zhang,Qi Luo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数自由的通用梯度滑动算法（PFUGS），用于求解凸复合优化问题，无需预先知道问题的常数参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度滑动算法通常需要预先知道问题的常数参数（如Hölder指数ν、常数M_ν和Lipschitz常数L），这在实践中往往难以获得。本文旨在开发一种参数自由的算法，无需任何先验知识就能高效求解凸复合优化问题。

Method: 提出了参数自由通用梯度滑动算法（PFUGS），该算法结合了梯度滑动框架和参数自适应技术。算法通过动态调整步长和其他参数来适应未知的问题常数，避免了需要预先指定这些参数的要求。

Result: 当f具有(M_ν,ν)-Hölder连续（次）梯度，g具有L-Lipschitz连续（次）梯度时，PFUGS算法分别需要最多O((M_ν/ε)^{2/(1+3ν)})和O((L/ε)^{1/2})次（次）梯度计算来获得近似解。该算法是参数自由的，不需要预先知道ν、M_ν和L。

Conclusion: PFUGS是第一个针对涉及两个具有不同问题常数的函数的参数自由梯度滑动算法，解决了现有方法需要先验知识的问题，具有更好的实用性和适应性。

Abstract: We propose a parameter-free universal gradient sliding (PFUGS) algorithm for computing an approximation solution to the convex composite optimization problem $\min_{x\in X} \{f(x) + g(x)\}$. When $f$ and $g$ have $(M_ν,ν)$-Hölder and $L$-Lipschitz continuous (sub)gradients respectively, our proposed PFUGS method computes an approximate solution within at most $\mathcal{O}((M_ν/\varepsilon)^{{2}/{(1+3ν)}})$ and $\mathcal{O}((L/\varepsilon)^{1/2})$ evaluations of (sub)gradients of $f$ and $g$ respectively. Moreover, the PFUGS algorithm is parameter-free and does not require any prior knowledge on problem constants $ν$, $M_ν$, and $L$. To the best of knowledge, for problems involving two functions with different sets of problem constants, PFUGS is the first gradient sliding algorithm that is parameter-free.

</details>

### [58] [Beyond Hard Constraints: Budget-Conditioned Reachability For Safe Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22292)
*Janaka Chathuranga Brahmanage,Akshat Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于安全可达性分析的离线安全强化学习方法，通过定义安全条件可达集来解耦奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束，避免不稳定的min/max对抗优化，实现从固定数据集学习安全策略而无需环境交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界任务需要在奖励最大化与安全约束之间取得平衡，传统方法常导致不稳定的min/max对抗优化。现有可达性分析方法主要处理硬安全约束，很少扩展到累积成本约束，需要一种能处理累积安全约束且避免不稳定优化的方法。

Method: 1) 定义安全条件可达集，将奖励最大化与累积安全成本约束解耦；2) 利用该集合在不使用min/max或拉格朗日优化的情况下强制执行安全约束；3) 开发基于此的离线安全RL算法，从固定数据集学习安全策略而无需环境交互。

Result: 在标准离线安全RL基准测试和真实世界海上导航任务中，该方法匹配或优于最先进的基线方法，同时保持安全性。

Conclusion: 提出的安全条件可达集方法有效解决了累积安全约束问题，避免了传统方法的不稳定性，为离线安全RL提供了一种有效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential decision making using Markov Decision Process underpins many realworld applications. Both model-based and model free methods have achieved strong results in these settings. However, real-world tasks must balance reward maximization with safety constraints, often conflicting objectives, that can lead to unstable min/max, adversarial optimization. A promising alternative is safety reachability analysis, which precomputes a forward-invariant safe state, action set, ensuring that an agent starting inside this set remains safe indefinitely. Yet, most reachability based methods address only hard safety constraints, and little work extends reachability to cumulative cost constraints. To address this, first, we define a safetyconditioned reachability set that decouples reward maximization from cumulative safety cost constraints. Second, we show how this set enforces safety constraints without unstable min/max or Lagrangian optimization, yielding a novel offline safe RL algorithm that learns a safe policy from a fixed dataset without environment interaction. Finally, experiments on standard offline safe RL benchmarks, and a real world maritime navigation task demonstrate that our method matches or outperforms state of the art baselines while maintaining safety.

</details>


### [59] [Efficient Embedding-based Synthetic Data Generation for Complex Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22294)
*Srideepika Jayaraman,Achille Fokoue,Dhaval Patel,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种基于嵌入空间的针对性采样管道，通过分析生成数据在嵌入空间中的分布，利用邻域密度与预测准确性的强相关性来提升数据多样性和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用大语言模型生成合成数据来微调小型模型已成为有效方法，但关键挑战在于确保生成数据的质量和多样性。现有方法在数据多样性方面存在不足，需要更系统的方法来评估和提升生成数据的分布特性。

Method: 提出基于嵌入空间的针对性采样管道：首先分析生成数据在嵌入空间中的多样性和分布，发现特定邻域内样本密度与该区域样本预测准确性存在强相关性；基于这一洞察，设计嵌入空间采样策略，通过选择性地采样低密度区域的数据来增强多样性。

Result: 该方法在多个基准测试中一致提升了性能，证明了基于嵌入空间的分析和采样策略能够有效增强数据多样性，从而改善小型语言模型的微调效果。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间分析为合成数据生成提供了有价值的洞察，基于邻域密度与预测准确性相关性的针对性采样策略能够有效提升数据多样性，为改进合成数据生成方法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Synthetic Data Generation (SDG), leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs), has recently been recognized and broadly adopted as an effective approach to improve the performance of smaller but more resource and compute efficient LLMs through fine-tuning. A key challenge in SDG is ensuring the quality and diversity of the generated data. In this paper, we analyze the diversity and distribution of generated data in the embedding space, and demonstrate a strong correlation between the density of examples within a specific neighborhood and the accuracy of predictions on examples drawn from that region. Building on this insight, we present a targeted pipeline for embedding-based sampling that enhances data diversity and consistently improves performance across several benchmarks.

</details>


### [60] [Between the Layers Lies the Truth: Uncertainty Estimation in LLMs Using Intra-Layer Local Information Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22299)
*Zvi N. Badash,Yonatan Belinkov,Moti Freiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于内部表示跨层一致性模式的紧凑型不确定性估计方法，在单次前向传播中计算，在跨数据集迁移和量化场景下优于传统探测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型经常表现出过度自信的错误，需要可靠的不确定性估计。基于输出的启发式方法廉价但脆弱，而探测内部表示的方法虽然有效但维度高且难以迁移。

Method: 提出一种紧凑的、针对每个实例的不确定性估计方法，通过单次前向传播对内部表示的跨层一致性模式进行评分。该方法分析不同层之间表示的一致性程度来估计模型不确定性。

Result: 在三个模型上的实验表明：在分布内匹配探测方法性能（AUPRC差异最大-1.8%，Brier分数差异+4.9点）；在跨数据集迁移中一致优于探测方法（AUPRC提升最高+2.86，Brier分数提升最高+21.02）；在4比特权重量化下保持鲁棒性（平均AUPRC提升+1.94，Brier分数提升+5.33）。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种轻量级、紧凑的方式来捕获大语言模型中的可迁移不确定性。通过分析特定的层间交互，揭示了不同模型编码不确定性的差异，为理解模型内部不确定性表征提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often confidently wrong, making reliable uncertainty estimation (UE) essential. Output-based heuristics are cheap but brittle, while probing internal representations is effective yet high-dimensional and hard to transfer.
  We propose a compact, per-instance UE method that scores cross-layer agreement patterns in internal representations using a single forward pass.
  Across three models, our method matches probing in-distribution, with mean diagonal differences of at most $-1.8$ AUPRC percentage points and $+4.9$ Brier score points. Under cross-dataset transfer, it consistently outperforms probing, achieving off-diagonal gains up to $+2.86$ AUPRC and $+21.02$ Brier points. Under 4-bit weight-only quantization, it remains robust, improving over probing by $+1.94$ AUPRC points and $+5.33$ Brier points on average.
  Beyond performance, examining specific layer--layer interactions reveals differences in how disparate models encode uncertainty. Altogether, our UE method offers a lightweight, compact means to capture transferable uncertainty in LLMs.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling Attention via Feature Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22300)
*Yan Xie,Tiansheng Wen,Tangda Huang,Bo Chen,Chenyu You,Stefanie Jegelka,Yifei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Sparse Feature Attention (SFA) 通过特征稀疏性将注意力计算成本从 O(n²d) 降低到 O(n²k²/d)，结合 FlashSFA 内核实现高效长上下文扩展


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通过局部窗口、核近似或令牌级稀疏性降低注意力计算成本，但这些方法会降低准确性。本文探索特征稀疏性这一正交维度，在保持高维表达能力的同时显著降低计算开销

Method: 提出 Sparse Feature Attention (SFA)，将查询和键表示为 k-稀疏编码，将注意力成本从 Θ(n²d) 降低到 Θ(n²k²/d)。开发 FlashSFA 内核，直接在稀疏重叠上操作而无需物化密集得分矩阵

Result: 在 GPT-2 和 Qwen3 预训练中，SFA 匹配密集基线，速度提升高达 2.5 倍，FLOPs 和 KV-cache 减少近 50%。在合成和下游基准测试中，SFA 在长上下文中保持检索准确性和鲁棒性

Conclusion: 特征级稀疏性是高效注意力的补充且未被充分探索的维度，使 Transformer 能够以最小质量损失扩展到数量级更长的上下文

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to ultra-long contexts is bottlenecked by the $O(n^2 d)$ cost of self-attention. Existing methods reduce this cost along the sequence axis through local windows, kernel approximations, or token-level sparsity, but these approaches consistently degrade accuracy. In this paper, we instead explore an orthogonal axis: feature sparsity. We propose Sparse Feature Attention (SFA), where queries and keys are represented as $k$-sparse codes that preserve high-dimensional expressivity while reducing the cost of attention from $Θ(n^2 d)$ to $Θ(n^2 k^2/d)$. To make this efficient at scale, we introduce FlashSFA, an IO-aware kernel that extends FlashAttention to operate directly on sparse overlaps without materializing dense score matrices. Across GPT-2 and Qwen3 pretraining, SFA matches dense baselines while improving speed by up to $2.5\times$ and reducing FLOPs and KV-cache by nearly 50\%. On synthetic and downstream benchmarks, SFA preserves retrieval accuracy and robustness at long contexts, outperforming short-embedding baselines that collapse feature diversity. These results establish feature-level sparsity as a complementary and underexplored axis for efficient attention, enabling Transformers to scale to orders-of-magnitude longer contexts with minimal quality loss. Code is available at https://github.com/YannX1e/Sparse-Feature-Attention.

</details>


### [62] [Latent Semantic Manifolds in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22301)
*Mohamed A. Mabrok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个数学框架，将LLM隐藏状态解释为潜在语义流形上的点，其中令牌对应划分流形的Voronoi区域，并定义了表达性间隙来衡量词汇离散化导致的语义失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在连续向量空间中进行内部计算，但产生离散令牌，这种基本不匹配的几何后果尚未被充分理解。需要建立数学框架来理解LLM隐藏状态的几何结构及其与词汇离散化的关系。

Method: 开发了一个数学框架，将LLM隐藏状态解释为配备Fisher信息度量的黎曼子流形上的点，其中令牌对应划分流形的Voronoi区域。定义了表达性间隙作为词汇离散化导致的语义失真度量，并证明了两个定理：有限词汇的失真率失真下界，以及通过余面积公式的表达性间隙线性体积缩放定律。在六个Transformer架构（124M-1.5B参数）上验证了预测。

Result: 验证了普遍的沙漏形内在维度分布、平滑曲率结构，以及斜率为0.87-1.12（R^2 > 0.985）的线性间隙缩放。跨模型的边界分布揭示了与规模无关的边界邻近表示的持久硬核，提供了困惑度的几何分解。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解LLM的几何结构提供了理论基础，揭示了词汇离散化导致的语义失真规律。研究结果对架构设计、模型压缩、解码策略和缩放定律具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform internal computations in continuous vector spaces yet produce discrete tokens -- a fundamental mismatch whose geometric consequences remain poorly understood. We develop a mathematical framework that interprets LLM hidden states as points on a latent semantic manifold: a Riemannian submanifold equipped with the Fisher information metric, where tokens correspond to Voronoi regions partitioning the manifold. We define the expressibility gap, a geometric measure of the semantic distortion from vocabulary discretization, and prove two theorems: a rate-distortion lower bound on distortion for any finite vocabulary, and a linear volume scaling law for the expressibility gap via the coarea formula. We validate these predictions across six transformer architectures (124M-1.5B parameters), confirming universal hourglass intrinsic dimension profiles, smooth curvature structure, and linear gap scaling with slopes 0.87-1.12 (R^2 > 0.985). The margin distribution across models reveals a persistent hard core of boundary-proximal representations invariant to scale, providing a geometric decomposition of perplexity. We discuss implications for architecture design, model compression, decoding strategies, and scaling laws

</details>


### [63] [Research on Individual Trait Clustering and Development Pathway Adaptation Based on the K-means Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22302)
*Qianru Wei,Jihaoyu Yang,Cheng Zhang,Jinming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用K-means聚类算法分析大学生CET-4成绩、GPA、人格特质和学生干部经历等数据，将学生分为四类群体，为不同特征组合的学生提供精准的职业指导建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有职业指导方法多关注职业路径预测，但较少研究不同特征组合学生在特定职业方向上的适配性。随着信息技术发展，人工智能和机器学习在教育领域应用潜力巨大，本研究旨在探索利用K-means聚类算法为大学生提供精准职业指导。

Method: 收集3000多名学生的CET-4成绩、GPA、人格特质和学生干部经历数据，采用K-means聚类算法将学生分为四个主要群体。该算法通过最小化簇内平方误差，将具有相似特征的学生归为一组，确保同一簇内学生特征高度相似，不同簇间差异最大化。

Result: 研究结果显示，具有不同特征组合的学生适合不同的职业方向。基于聚类结果，为每个群体提供了针对性的职业指导建议，为个性化职业指导提供了科学依据，有效提升了学生的就业成功率。

Conclusion: K-means聚类算法能够有效识别学生特征模式并为个性化职业指导提供科学依据。未来研究可通过扩大样本规模、增加特征变量和考虑外部因素，进一步提高聚类精度和指导效果。

Abstract: With the development of information technology, the application of artificial intelligence and machine learning in the field of education shows great potential. This study aims to explore how to utilize K-means clustering algorithm to provide accurate career guidance for college students. Existing methods mostly focus on the prediction of career paths, but there are fewer studies on the fitness of students with different combinations of characteristics in specific career directions. In this study, we analyze the data of more than 3000 students on their CET-4 scores, GPA, personality traits and student cadre experiences, and use the K-means clustering algorithm to classify the students into four main groups. The K-means clustering algorithm groups students with similar characteristics into one group by minimizing the intra-cluster squared error, ensuring that the students within the same cluster are highly similar in their characteristics, and that differences between different clusters are maximized. Based on the clustering results, targeted career guidance suggestions are provided for each group. The results of the study show that students with different combinations of characteristics are suitable for different career directions, which provides a scientific basis for personalized career guidance and effectively enhances students' employment success rate. Future research can further improve the precision of clustering and the guidance effect by expanding the sample size, increasing the feature variables and considering external factors.

</details>


### [64] [Sample Transform Cost-Based Training-Free Hallucination Detector for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22303)
*Zeyang Ding,Xinglin Hu,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优传输距离的LLM幻觉检测方法，通过计算样本间Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化条件分布的复杂度，无需训练即可检测幻觉


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中的幻觉问题是可信部署的主要障碍，需要准确、轻量且广泛适用的检测器。LLM在给定提示下定义的条件分布复杂度可作为幻觉的指示器，但分布密度未知且样本是离散分布，量化复杂度面临挑战

Method: 计算成对样本的token嵌入集合之间的最优传输距离，得到Wasserstein距离矩阵来量化LLM条件分布的复杂度。基于该矩阵推导两个互补信号：AvgWD（平均成本）和EigenWD（成本复杂度）。进一步通过可访问的教师模型进行teacher forcing，将框架扩展到黑盒LLM

Result: 实验表明AvgWD和EigenWD与强不确定性基线方法具有竞争力，在不同模型和数据集上表现出互补行为，证明分布复杂度是LLM真实性的有效信号

Conclusion: 基于最优传输距离的分布复杂度量化方法为LLM幻觉检测提供了有效的训练免费检测器，能够准确评估模型输出的真实性，具有广泛适用性

Abstract: Hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) remain a central obstacle to trustworthy deployment, motivating detectors that are accurate, lightweight, and broadly applicable. Since an LLM with a prompt defines a conditional distribution, we argue that the complexity of the distribution is an indicator of hallucination. However, the density of the distribution is unknown and the samples (i.e., responses generated for the prompt) are discrete distributions, which leads to a significant challenge in quantifying the complexity of the distribution. We propose to compute the optimal-transport distances between the sets of token embeddings of pairwise samples, which yields a Wasserstein distance matrix measuring the costs of transforming between the samples. This Wasserstein distance matrix provides a means to quantify the complexity of the distribution defined by the LLM with the prompt. Based on the Wasserstein distance matrix, we derive two complementary signals: AvgWD, measuring the average cost, and EigenWD, measuring the cost complexity. This leads to a training-free detector for hallucinations in LLMs. We further extend the framework to black-box LLMs via teacher forcing with an accessible teacher model. Experiments show that AvgWD and EigenWD are competitive with strong uncertainty baselines and provide complementary behavior across models and datasets, highlighting distribution complexity as an effective signal for LLM truthfulness.

</details>


### [65] [Mitigating Premature Discretization with Progressive Quantization for Robust Vector Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22304)
*Wenhao Zhao,Qiran Zou,Zhouhan Lin,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProVQ通过渐进量化解决传统VQ中的"过早离散化"问题，在图像和蛋白质序列建模中取得显著性能提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量量化方法存在"过早离散化"的根本冲突：在编码器捕获底层数据流形之前就强制离散化，这限制了模型性能

Method: 提出渐进量化方法，将量化硬度动态作为VQ训练的基本轴，通过从连续潜在空间平滑退火到离散空间的课程学习，引导码本朝向充分扩展的流形

Result: 在ImageNet-1K和ImageNet-100基准测试中改善了重建和生成性能；在蛋白质结构标记化方面，在StrutTokenBench排行榜上建立了新的性能上限

Conclusion: ProVQ通过解决过早离散化问题，在多模态建模中展现出广泛有效性，为向量量化提供了新的训练范式

Abstract: Vector Quantization (VQ) has become the cornerstone of tokenization for many multimodal Large Language Models and diffusion synthesis. However, existing VQ paradigms suffer from a fundamental conflict: they enforce discretization before the encoder has captured the underlying data manifold. We term this phenomenon Premature Discretization. To resolve this, we propose Progressive Quantization (ProVQ), which incorporates the dynamics of quantization hardness as a fundamental yet previously overlooked axis in VQ training. By treating quantization as a curriculum that smoothly anneals from a continuous latent space to a discrete one, ProVQ effectively guides the codebook toward the well-expanded manifolds. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the broad effectiveness of ProVQ across diverse modalities. We report improved reconstruction and generative performance on the ImageNet-1K and ImageNet-100 benchmarks, highlighting the ProVQ's boost for generative modeling. Furthermore, ProVQ proves highly effective for modeling complex biological sequences, establishing a new performance ceiling for protein structure tokenization on the StrutTokenBench leaderboard.

</details>


### [66] [UniFluids: Unified Neural Operator Learning with Conditional Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22309)
*Haosen Li,Qi Meng,Jiahao Li,Rui Zhang,Ruihua Song,Liang Ma,Zhi-Ming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniFluids：基于条件流匹配和扩散Transformer的统一PDE求解算子学习框架，支持多种维度和物理变量的并行序列生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDE模拟在科学计算中具有重要意义，深度学习为学习PDE求解算子提供了新机遇。现有方法多为自回归模型，缺乏统一的跨维度、跨物理变量的并行生成框架。

Method: 提出UniFluids框架：1）采用条件流匹配实现并行序列生成；2）利用扩散Transformer的可扩展性；3）引入统一的四维时空表示处理异构PDE数据集；4）使用x-prediction策略提升预测精度。

Result: 在1D、2D、3D空间维度的多个PDE数据集上进行了大规模评估，UniFluids展现出强大的预测精度、良好的可扩展性和跨场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniFluids是首个基于流匹配的统一PDE求解算子学习框架，通过并行生成、统一表示和x-prediction策略，在多个维度上实现了优异的性能。

Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) simulation holds extensive significance in scientific research. Currently, the integration of deep neural networks to learn solution operators of PDEs has introduced great potential. In this paper, we present UniFluids, a conditional flow-matching framework that harnesses the scalability of diffusion Transformer to unify learning of solution operators across diverse PDEs with varying dimensionality and physical variables. Unlike the autoregressive PDE foundation models, UniFluids adopts flow-matching to achieve parallel sequence generation, making it the first such approach for unified operator learning. Specifically, the introduction of a unified four-dimensional spatiotemporal representation for the heterogeneous PDE datasets enables joint training and conditional encoding. Furthermore, we find the effective dimension of the PDE dataset is much lower than its patch dimension. We thus employ $x$-prediction in the flow-matching operator learning, which is verified to significantly improve prediction accuracy. We conduct a large-scale evaluation of UniFluids on several PDE datasets covering spatial dimensions 1D, 2D and 3D. Experimental results show that UniFluids achieves strong prediction accuracy and demonstrates good scalability and cross-scenario generalization capability. The code will be released later.

</details>


### [67] [A Multi-Modal CNN-LSTM Framework with Multi-Head Attention and Focal Loss for Real-Time Elderly Fall Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22313)
*Lijie Zhou,Luran Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MultiModalFallDetector多模态深度学习框架，用于老年人实时跌倒检测，融合加速度计、陀螺仪和生理信号，通过多尺度CNN、自注意力机制等技术，在SisFall数据集上达到98.7% F1分数，推理延迟低于50ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球老龄化加剧了对可靠健康监测系统的需求，特别是老年人跌倒检测。传统基于单模态加速度数据的方法误报率高，传统机器学习方法需要大量手工特征工程。

Method: 提出MultiModalFallDetector多模态深度学习框架：1) 多尺度CNN特征提取器捕捉不同时间分辨率的运动动态；2) 融合三轴加速度计、陀螺仪和四通道生理信号；3) 多头自注意力机制进行动态时间加权；4) 采用Focal Loss缓解严重类别不平衡；5) 引入辅助活动分类任务进行正则化；6) 从UCI HAR到SisFall数据集的迁移学习。

Result: 在SisFall数据集（包含60-85岁老年人真实模拟跌倒试验）上，框架达到F1分数98.7%、召回率98.9%、AUC-ROC 99.4%，显著优于传统机器学习和标准深度学习方法。在边缘设备上推理延迟低于50ms。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态深度学习框架在老年人跌倒检测方面表现出色，具有高准确性和实时性，适合在老年护理环境中部署。

Abstract: The increasing global aging population has intensified the demand for reliable health monitoring systems, particularly those capable of detecting critical events such as falls among elderly individuals. Traditional fall detection approaches relying on single-modality acceleration data suffer from high false alarm rates, while conventional machine learning methods require extensive hand-crafted feature engineering. This paper proposes a novel multi-modal deep learning framework, MultiModalFallDetector, designed for real-time elderly fall detection using wearable sensors. Our approach integrates multiple innovations: a multi-scale CNN-based feature extractor capturing motion dynamics at varying temporal resolutions; fusion of tri-axial accelerometer, gyroscope, and four-channel physiological signals; incorporation of a multi-head self-attention mechanism for dynamic temporal weighting; adoption of Focal Loss to mitigate severe class imbalance; introduction of an auxiliary activity classification task for regularization; and implementation of transfer learning from UCI HAR to SisFall dataset. Extensive experiments on the SisFall dataset, which includes real-world simulated fall trials from elderly participants (aged 60-85), demonstrate that our framework achieves an F1-score of 98. 7, Recall of 98. 9, and AUC-ROC of 99. 4, significantly outperforming baseline methods including traditional machine learning and standard deep learning approaches. The model maintains sub- 50ms inference latency on edge devices, confirming its suitability for real-time deployment in geriatric care settings.

</details>


### [68] [Emergency Preemption Without Online Exploration: A Decision Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22315)
*Haoran Su,Hanxiao Deng,Yandong Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于决策变换器的返回条件框架，用于优化应急车辆走廊，通过离线序列建模实现调度级紧急控制，无需在线环境交互，并在多智能体设置中扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应急车辆响应时间是生存结果的关键决定因素，但现有的信号优先策略仍然是反应式且不可控的，需要更智能、可控的优化方法。

Method: 将走廊优化建模为离线、返回条件的序列建模问题，使用决策变换器框架，通过单一目标返回标量实现调度级紧急控制，并扩展到多智能体设置，采用带图注意力的多智能体决策变换器进行空间协调。

Result: 在LightSim模拟器上，DT在4x4网格上将平均应急车辆行驶时间减少37.7%（88.6秒 vs 142.3秒），实现最低的民用车辆延误（11.3秒/辆）和最少的应急车辆停车次数（1.2次）。MADT在8x8网格上进一步改进，通过图注意力协调实现45.2%的减少。返回条件产生平滑调度界面：目标返回从100到-400变化时，应急车辆行驶时间（72.4-138.2秒）与民用车辆延误（16.8-5.4秒/辆）之间可进行权衡，无需重新训练。

Conclusion: 提出的返回条件决策变换器框架为应急车辆走廊优化提供了有效解决方案，实现了离线学习、可控的紧急响应和可扩展的多智能体协调，显著优于现有方法，包括需要在线环境交互的强化学习基线。

Abstract: Emergency vehicle (EV) response time is a critical determinant of survival outcomes, yet deployed signal preemption strategies remain reactive and uncontrollable. We propose a return-conditioned framework for emergency corridor optimization based on the Decision Transformer (DT). By casting corridor optimization as offline, return-conditioned sequence modeling, our approach (1) eliminates online environment interaction during policy learning, (2) enables dispatch-level urgency control through a single target-return scalar, and (3) extends to multi-agent settings via a Multi-Agent Decision Transformer (MADT) with graph attention for spatial coordination. On the LightSim simulator, DT reduces average EV travel time by 37.7% relative to fixed-timing preemption on a 4x4 grid (88.6 s vs. 142.3 s), achieving the lowest civilian delay (11.3 s/veh) and fewest EV stops (1.2) among all methods, including online RL baselines that require environment interaction. MADT further improves on larger grids, overtaking DT with 45.2% reduction on 8x8 via graph-attention coordination. Return conditioning produces a smooth dispatch interface: varying the target return from 100 to -400 trades EV travel time (72.4-138.2 s) against civilian delay (16.8-5.4 s/veh), requiring no retraining. A Constrained DT extension adds explicit civilian disruption budgets as a second control knob.

</details>


### [69] [ST-GDance++: A Scalable Spatial-Temporal Diffusion for Long-Duration Group Choreography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22316)
*Jing Xu,Weiqiang Wang,Cunjian Chen,Jun Liu,Qiuhong Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ST-GDance++：一种可扩展的群体舞蹈生成框架，通过解耦空间和时间依赖关系，实现高效且避免碰撞的群体编舞生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体舞蹈生成模型在交互场景中部署困难，存在双向注意力依赖导致计算复杂度二次增长、运动碰撞风险增加、难以有效建模密集时空交互等问题，限制了可扩展性和多舞者协调稳定性

Method: 提出ST-GDance++框架，解耦空间和时间依赖：空间建模采用轻量级距离感知图卷积捕捉舞者间关系；时间建模设计扩散噪声调度策略和高效时间对齐注意力掩码，支持长序列流式生成

Result: 在AIOZ-GDance数据集上的实验表明，ST-GDance++在保持竞争性生成质量的同时，相比现有方法显著降低了延迟

Conclusion: ST-GDance++通过解耦时空依赖和高效建模策略，解决了群体舞蹈生成中的可扩展性和交互部署难题，为实际应用提供了可行方案

Abstract: Group dance generation from music requires synchronizing multiple dancers while maintaining spatial coordination, making it highly relevant to applications such as film production, gaming, and animation. Recent group dance generation models have achieved promising generation quality, but they remain difficult to deploy in interactive scenarios due to bidirectional attention dependencies. As the number of dancers and the sequence length increase, the attention computation required for aligning music conditions with motion sequences grows quadratically, leading to reduced efficiency and increased risk of motion collisions. Effectively modeling dense spatial-temporal interactions is therefore essential, yet existing methods often struggle to capture such complexity, resulting in limited scalability and unstable multi-dancer coordination. To address these challenges, we propose ST-GDance++, a scalable framework that decouples spatial and temporal dependencies to enable efficient and collision-aware group choreography generation. For spatial modeling, we introduce lightweight distance-aware graph convolutions to capture inter-dancer relationships while reducing computational overhead. For temporal modeling, we design a diffusion noise scheduling strategy together with an efficient temporal-aligned attention mask, enabling stream-based generation for long motion sequences and improving scalability in long-duration scenarios. Experiments on the AIOZ-GDance dataset show that ST-GDance++ achieves competitive generation quality with significantly reduced latency compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [70] [Towards The Implicit Bias on Multiclass Separable Data Under Norm Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22824)
*Shengping Xie,Zekun Wu,Quan Chen,Kaixu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NucGD优化器，通过核范数约束强制低秩结构，分析随机优化动态对多类可分数据隐式偏差的影响


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度算法在过参数化模型中的隐式偏差对泛化至关重要，但其机制复杂。需要研究优化几何如何影响多类可分数据上的解，并设计能强制低秩结构的几何感知优化器

Method: 基于归一化最陡下降(NSD)框架，提出NucGD优化器，通过核范数约束强制低秩结构；推导基于异步幂迭代的高效SVD-free更新规则；实证分析小批量采样和动量引起的梯度噪声对收敛到最大边际解的影响

Result: NucGD能有效强制低秩结构，与新兴低秩投影方法建立统一视角；异步幂迭代实现可扩展训练；揭示了梯度噪声水平如何调节向期望最大边际解的收敛

Conclusion: 通过几何感知优化器NucGD和随机优化动态分析，深化了对多类可分数据上隐式偏差机制的理解，为设计能诱导期望结构偏差的优化算法提供了新视角

Abstract: Implicit bias induced by gradient-based algorithms is essential to the generalization of overparameterized models, yet its mechanisms can be subtle. This work leverages the Normalized Steepest Descent} (NSD) framework to investigate how optimization geometry shapes solutions on multiclass separable data. We introduce NucGD, a geometry-aware optimizer designed to enforce low rank structures through nuclear norm constraints. Beyond the algorithm itself, we connect NucGD with emerging low-rank projection methods, providing a unified perspective. To enable scalable training, we derive an efficient SVD-free update rule via asynchronous power iteration. Furthermore, we empirically dissect the impact of stochastic optimization dynamics, characterizing how varying levels of gradient noise induced by mini-batch sampling and momentum modulate the convergence toward the expected maximum margin solutions.Our code is accessible at: https://github.com/Tsokarsic/observing-the-implicit-bias-on-multiclass-seperable-data.

</details>


### [71] [Bridging the Gap Between Climate Science and Machine Learning in Climate Model Emulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22320)
*Luca Schmidt,Nina Effenberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个框架来克服气候模型ML模拟器的使用障碍，通过整合气候科学和机器学习视角，设计易于采用且可靠的模拟器


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候模型计算和技术需求高，ML模拟器虽然能降低计算成本但实际使用面临多重障碍：可访问性有限、缺乏专业知识、对ML方法物理充分性不信任

Method: 引入整合气候科学和机器学习视角的框架，设计易于采用的模拟器，针对明确定义的任务，并证明其可靠性

Result: 发现设计易于采用、针对明确任务且证明可靠的模拟器是弥合气候科学和机器学习领域差距的有希望途径

Conclusion: 通过整合双重视角和设计用户友好的可靠模拟器，可以有效克服气候模型ML模拟器的使用障碍，促进跨领域合作

Abstract: While climate models provide insights for climate decision-making, their use is constrained by significant computational and technical demands. Although machine learning (ML) emulators offer a way to bypass the high computational costs, their effective use remains challenging. The hurdles are diverse, ranging from limited accessibility and a lack of specialized knowledge to a general mistrust of ML methods that are perceived as insufficiently physical. Here, we introduce a framework to overcome these barriers by integrating both climate science and machine learning perspectives. We find that designing easy-to-adopt emulators that address a clearly defined task and demonstrating their reliability offers a promising path for bridging the gap between our two fields.

</details>


### [72] [A Learning Method with Gap-Aware Generation for Heterogeneous DAG Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23249)
*Ruisong Zhou,Haijun Zou,Li Zhou,Chumin Sun,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeCAN：基于强化学习的端到端异构DAG调度框架，采用两阶段单次前向设计，通过加权交叉注意力编码器建模任务-资源池交互，并引入跳转扩展机制消除生成诱导的最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构环境中DAG调度面临资源容量和依赖关系的挑战，需要适应不同资源池和任务类型的环境变化，同时要求快速生成调度方案。传统列表调度方法存在生成诱导的最优性差距问题。

Method: 提出WeCAN框架：1）采用两阶段单次前向设计，一次前向传播生成任务-资源池评分和全局参数；2）使用加权交叉注意力编码器建模任务-资源池交互，通过兼容性系数门控；3）引入顺序空间分析，表征生成映射的可达调度顺序集；4）设计跳转扩展实现，采用分析参数化的递减跳转规则扩大可达顺序集。

Result: 在计算图和真实TPC-H DAG上的实验表明，WeCAN相比强基线改进了完工时间，推理时间与经典启发式算法相当，且比多轮神经调度器更快。

Conclusion: WeCAN通过端到端强化学习框架解决了异构DAG调度中的环境适应性和最优性差距问题，单次前向设计实现了高效调度生成，跳转扩展机制有效扩大了可达调度空间。

Abstract: Efficient scheduling of directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) in heterogeneous environments is challenging due to resource capacities and dependencies. In practice, the need for adaptability across environments with varying resource pools and task types, alongside rapid schedule generation, complicates these challenges. We propose WeCAN, an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework for heterogeneous DAG scheduling that addresses task--pool compatibility coefficients and generation-induced optimality gaps. It adopts a two-stage single-pass design: a single forward pass produces task--pool scores and global parameters, followed by a generation map that constructs schedules without repeated network calls. Its weighted cross-attention encoder models task--pool interactions gated by compatibility coefficients, and is size-agnostic to environment fluctuations. Moreover, widely used list-scheduling maps can incur generation-induced optimality gaps from restricted reachability. We introduce an order-space analysis that characterizes the reachable set of generation maps via feasible schedule orders, explains the mechanism behind generation-induced gaps, and yields sufficient conditions for gap elimination. Guided by these conditions, we design a skip-extended realization with an analytically parameterized decreasing skip rule, which enlarges the reachable order set while preserving single-pass efficiency. Experiments on computation graphs and real-world TPC-H DAGs demonstrate improved makespan over strong baselines, with inference time comparable to classical heuristics and faster than multi-round neural schedulers.

</details>


### [73] [AEGIS: An Operational Infrastructure for Post-Market Governance of Adaptive Medical AI Under US and EU Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22322)
*Fardin Afdideh,Mehdi Astaraki,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AEGIS是一个医疗AI治理框架，将FDA和欧盟的监管要求转化为可执行程序，支持安全的持续学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗设备中的机器学习系统需要治理框架来确保安全同时支持持续改进，FDA和欧盟的监管机制需要可操作的实施方案

Method: 提出AEGIS框架，包含三个模块：数据集同化和重训练、模型监控、条件决策，实现四类部署决策分类和独立的PMS ALARM信号

Result: 在脓毒症预测案例的11次模拟迭代中，AEGIS产生了8次批准、1次有条件批准、1次临床审查和1次拒绝，ALARM信号在第8和10次迭代触发，在性能下降前检测到漂移

Conclusion: AEGIS成功将监管变更控制概念转化为可执行的治理程序，支持跨不同临床应用的适应性医疗AI的安全持续学习

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in medical devices require governance frameworks that ensure safety while enabling continuous improvement. Regulatory bodies including the FDA and European Union have introduced mechanisms such as the Predetermined Change Control Plan (PCCP) and Post-Market Surveillance (PMS) to manage iterative model updates without repeated submissions. This paper presents AI/ML Evaluation and Governance Infrastructure for Safety (AEGIS), a governance framework applicable to any healthcare AI system. AEGIS comprises three modules, i.e., dataset assimilation and retraining, model monitoring, and conditional decision, that operationalize FDA PCCP and EU AI Act Article 43(4) provisions. We implement a four-category deployment decision taxonomy (APPROVE, CONDITIONAL APPROVAL, CLINICAL REVIEW, REJECT) with an independent PMS ALARM signal, enabling detection of the critical state in which no deployable model exists while the released model is simultaneously at risk.
  To illustrate how AEGIS can be instantiated across heterogeneous clinical contexts, we provide two examples: sepsis prediction from electronic health records and brain tumor segmentation from medical imaging. Both cases use identical governance architecture, differing only in configuration. Across 11 simulated iterations on the sepsis example, AEGIS yielded 8 APPROVE, 1 CONDITIONAL APPROVAL, 1 CLINICAL REVIEW, and 1 REJECT decision, exercising all four categories. ALARM signals were co-issued at iterations 8 and 10, including the critical state where no deployable model exists and the released model is simultaneously failing. AEGIS detected drift before observable performance degradation. These results demonstrate that AEGIS translates regulatory change-control concepts into executable governance procedures, supporting safe continuous learning for adaptive medical AI across diverse clinical applications.

</details>


### [74] [Byzantine-Robust and Differentially Private Federated Optimization under Weaker Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23472)
*Rustem Islamov,Grigory Malinovsky,Alexander Gaponov,Aurelien Lucchi,Peter Richtárik,Eduard Gorbunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Byz-Clip21-SGD2M算法，在联邦学习中统一解决差分隐私和拜占庭鲁棒性问题，提供收敛保证并改进现有方法的局限性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中梯度泄露敏感信息和恶意服务器攻击（拜占庭攻击）的风险，需要统一解决差分隐私和拜占庭鲁棒性。现有方法存在不现实假设、需要辅助数据集或缺乏收敛保证等局限性

Method: 提出Byz-Clip21-SGD2M算法，结合鲁棒聚合、双动量和精心设计的裁剪技术。在标准L-光滑性和σ-次高斯梯度噪声假设下证明高概率收敛保证

Result: 在无攻击者情况下恢复最先进收敛率，在拜占庭和差分隐私设置下改进效用保证。在MNIST数据集上使用CNN和MLP模型的实证评估验证了方法的有效性

Conclusion: Byz-Clip21-SGD2M算法成功解决了联邦学习中差分隐私和拜占庭鲁棒性的统一框架问题，放宽了先前工作的条件限制，提供了理论保证和实证验证

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables heterogeneous clients to collaboratively train a shared model without centralizing their raw data, offering an inherent level of privacy. However, gradients and model updates can still leak sensitive information, while malicious servers may mount adversarial attacks such as Byzantine manipulation. These vulnerabilities highlight the need to address differential privacy (DP) and Byzantine robustness within a unified framework. Existing approaches, however, often rely on unrealistic assumptions such as bounded gradients, require auxiliary server-side datasets, or fail to provide convergence guarantees. We address these limitations by proposing Byz-Clip21-SGD2M, a new algorithm that integrates robust aggregation with double momentum and carefully designed clipping. We prove high-probability convergence guarantees under standard $L$-smoothness and $σ$-sub-Gaussian gradient noise assumptions, thereby relaxing conditions that dominate prior work. Our analysis recovers state-of-the-art convergence rates in the absence of adversaries and improves utility guarantees under Byzantine and DP settings. Empirical evaluations on CNN and MLP models trained on MNIST further validate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [75] [DAQ: Delta-Aware Quantization for Post-Training LLM Weight Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22324)
*Xiaoming Yu,Shize Tang,Guanghua Yu,Linchuan Xie,Song Liu,Jianchen Zhu,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DAQ是一种数据无关的后训练量化框架，通过保护后训练获得的参数变化来保持模型能力，使用符号保持率和余弦相似度替代传统重建误差目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化方法最小化重建误差但忽略基础模型，导致量化噪声不成比例地破坏编码后训练行为的小幅度参数变化(ΔW)，从而损害后训练获得的能力。

Method: 提出Delta-Aware Quantization (DAQ)框架，用两个ΔW感知的度量指标替代基于重建的目标：符号保持率(Sign Preservation Rate)和余弦相似度(Cosine Similarity)，直接优化ΔW的方向保真度，仅需基础和训练后权重矩阵。

Result: 在FP8试点研究中，DAQ恢复了标准量化下丢失的风格特定能力，同时保持一般性能。

Conclusion: DAQ通过直接保护后训练参数变化的方向保真度，有效解决了传统量化方法破坏后训练获得能力的问题，为数据无关的后训练量化提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Delta-Aware Quantization (DAQ), a data-free post-training quantization framework that preserves the knowledge acquired during post-training. Standard quantization objectives minimize reconstruction error but are agnostic to the base model, allowing quantization noise to disproportionately corrupt the small-magnitude parameter deltas ($ΔW$) that encode post-training behavior -- an effect we analyze through the lens of quantization as implicit regularization. DAQ replaces reconstruction-based objectives with two delta-aware metrics -- Sign Preservation Rate and Cosine Similarity -- that directly optimize for directional fidelity of $ΔW$, requiring only the base and post-trained weight matrices. In a pilot FP8 study, DAQ recovers style-specific capabilities lost under standard quantization while maintaining general performance.

</details>


### [76] [Hybrid Associative Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22325)
*Leon Lufkin,Tomás Figliolia,Beren Millidge,Kamesh Krishnamurthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HAM层结合RNN和自注意力机制，RNN压缩整个序列，注意力仅补充RNN难以预测的信息，实现KV缓存的数据依赖增长和用户可控调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN和自注意力机制在序列建模中各有优缺点：RNN将整个过去压缩为固定大小状态，效率高但长上下文性能下降；自注意力存储每个过去时间步，KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，信息检索能力强但计算内存成本高。现有工作简单交替使用这两种机制以降低计算成本，但未充分利用它们的互补性。

Method: 提出混合关联记忆（HAM）层，结合RNN和自注意力机制：RNN压缩整个序列，注意力机制仅补充RNN难以预测的信息（最有价值的信息）。HAM层实现KV缓存的数据依赖增长，用户可通过单一连续阈值精确控制缓存增长率。

Result: KV缓存增长率与损失和性能之间存在平滑的权衡关系。实验表明，混合架构在显著降低KV缓存使用量的情况下，相对于RNN和Transformer仍能提供强大、有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: HAM层通过结合RNN的压缩能力和自注意力的精确检索能力，实现了两种机制的互补优势，提供了KV缓存增长率的细粒度控制，在效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) and self-attention are both widely used sequence-mixing layers that maintain an internal memory. However, this memory is constructed using two orthogonal mechanisms: RNNs compress the entire past into a fixed-size state, whereas self-attention's state stores every past time step growing its state (the KV cache) linearly with the sequence length. This results in orthogonal strengths and weaknesses. Self-attention layers excel at retrieving information in the context but have large memory and computational costs, while RNNs are more efficient but degrade over longer contexts and underperform for precise recall tasks. Prior work combining these mechanisms has focused primarily on naively interleaving them to reduce computational cost without regard to their complementary mechanisms. We propose the Hybrid Associative Memory (HAM) layer, which combines self-attention and RNNs while leveraging their individual strengths: the RNN compresses the entire sequence, while attention supplements it *only* with information that is difficult for the RNN to predict, which is hence the most valuable information to explicitly store. HAM layers enable data-dependent growth of the KV cache, which can be precisely controlled by the user with a single, continuous threshold. We find that this fine-grained control of the KV cache growth rate has a smooth trade-off with loss and performance. Empirically, we show that our hybrid architecture offers strong, competitive performance relative to RNNs and Transformers even at substantially lower KV-cache usage.

</details>


### [77] [A Direct Classification Approach for Reliable Wind Ramp Event Forecasting under Severe Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22326)
*Alejandro Morales-Hernández,Fabrizio De Caroa,Gian Marco Paldino,Pascal Tribel,Alfredo Vaccaro,Gianluca Bontempi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种针对风电功率爬坡事件预测的新方法，通过结合多数类欠采样和集成学习来解决类别不平衡问题，在真实数据集上取得了超过85%的准确率和88%的加权F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低碳电力系统中，决策支持系统对维持电网稳定至关重要，需要为控制室操作员提供风电功率爬坡事件的实时预警。然而，风电爬坡事件数据集存在严重的类别不平衡问题（爬坡事件通常少于观测事件的15%），传统机器学习模型会偏向多数类，导致预测性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的风电爬坡事件预测方法，将其构建为多元时间序列分类任务。采用数据预处理策略从近期功率观测中提取特征，并屏蔽不可用的爬坡信息，使其可与传统实时爬坡识别工具集成。核心方法是结合多数类欠采样和集成学习来增强类别不平衡下的风电爬坡事件预测。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的数值模拟表明，该方法优于基准分类器，取得了超过85%的准确率和88%的加权F1分数。

Conclusion: 所提出的结合多数类欠采样和集成学习的方法能有效解决风电爬坡事件预测中的类别不平衡问题，显著提升了预测性能，为电力系统决策支持提供了更可靠的预警工具。

Abstract: Decision support systems are essential for maintaining grid stability in low-carbon power systems, such as wind power plants, by providing real-time alerts to control room operators regarding potential events, including Wind Power Ramp Events (WPREs). These early warnings enable the timely initiation of more detailed system stability assessments and preventive actions. However, forecasting these events is challenging due to the inherent class imbalance in WPRE datasets, where ramp events are less frequent (typically less than 15\% of observed events) compared to normal conditions. Ignoring this characteristic undermines the performance of conventional machine learning models, which often favor the majority class. This paper introduces a novel methodology for WPRE forecasting as a multivariate time series classification task and proposes a data preprocessing strategy that extracts features from recent power observations and masks unavailable ramp information, making it integrable with traditional real-time ramp identification tools. Particularly, the proposed methodology combines majority-class undersampling and ensemble learning to enhance wind ramp event forecasting under class imbalance. Numerical simulations conducted on a real-world dataset demonstrate the superiority of our approach, achieving over 85% accuracy and 88% weighted F1 score, outperforming benchmark classifiers.

</details>


### [78] [Trained Persistent Memory for Frozen Decoder-Only LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22329)
*Hong Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探索了在仅解码器语言模型中实现持久潜在空间记忆的方法，通过训练小型记忆适配器使冻结的GPT-2具备跨会话记忆能力，发现了不同架构方法的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅解码器语言模型（如GPT-2）是无状态的，每次前向传播后隐藏表示都会被丢弃，缺乏跨会话的持久记忆。先前研究在编码器-解码器架构中通过记忆适配器实现了持久记忆，本研究旨在探索这一原理是否适用于仅解码器设置，其中没有交叉注意力路径，记忆必须通过自注意力单独注入。

Method: 将六种记忆方法（前缀、并行交叉注意力、KV扩展、Hebbian记忆、上下文门控分支和基于槽的稀疏写入）适配到冻结的GPT-2模型，仅训练小型记忆适配器θ_mem。写入规则共享，读取注入从解码器交叉注意力改为自注意力KV前缀或并行分支。在LoCoMo基准上测试不同容量（1×和10×）下的性能。
